- **bkn-creator**: 【首选技能】凡涉及知识网络的任何操作，优先使用 bkn-creator，不要直接触发 create-bkn。 bkn-creator 是 BKN 全生命周期管理器，负责流程路由识别、阶段门禁、子流程编排与结果回执。 覆盖新增、查找、更新、删除（CRUD）并采用渐进式执行。 触发词：创建知识网络、新建BKN、BKN建模、本体建模、对象类、关系类、动作类、 概念组、BKN文件、BKN push、BKN pull、对象类绑定、关系类映射、 对象类提取、关系类提取、实体关系抽取、 知识网络查询、知识网络更新、知识网络删除。 不应触发：仅查询平台功能、Agent 对话、Vega/Catalog 操作、 健康巡检、数据源连接器等非 BKN 生命周期操作时，应由 ontology-core 等其他技能处理。 ([UnicomAI/wanwu](https://github.com/UnicomAI/wanwu/tree/HEAD/configs/microservice/bff-service/configs/agent-skills/ontology/bkn-creator) · ★2.4k)
- **bkn-creator**: BKN 全生命周期编排入口。按需读取 OpenBKN SDK / CLI 操作参考。 凡涉及创建 BKN、从 PRD/文档提取对象关系、 生成或更新 `.bkn`、做数据视图绑定、环境检查、测试集生成、校验与推送时， 优先使用 bkn-creator 进行流程路由、阶段门禁、子 skill 编排与结果回执。 适用于 BKN 的 create/read/update/delete/extract/copy/validate 场景， 技能包能力补齐/skill 草案生成场景， 以及使用反馈巡检与改进场景（定时任务触发、Agent 对话质量异常、 feedback_brief 传入、知识网络持续优化）。 不应用于纯数据语义查询，该场景应交由 data-semantic 处理。 ([openbkn-ai/bkn-engineering](https://github.com/openbkn-ai/bkn-engineering/tree/HEAD/skills/bkn-creator) · ★27)
- **bkn-creator**: 【首选技能】凡涉及知识网络的任何操作，优先使用 bkn-creator，不要直接触发 create-bkn。 bkn-creator 是 KWeaver BKN 全生命周期管理器，负责流程路由识别、阶段门禁、子流程编排与结果回执。 覆盖新增、查找、更新、删除（CRUD）并采用渐进式执行。 触发词：创建知识网络、新建BKN、BKN建模、本体建模、对象类、关系类、动作类、 概念组、BKN文件、BKN push、BKN pull、对象类绑定、关系类映射、 对象类提取、关系类提取、实体关系抽取、 GKN视图匹配、知识网络查询、知识网络更新、知识网络删除。 不应触发：仅查询平台功能、Agent 对话、Vega/Catalog 操作、 健康巡检、数据源连接器等非 BKN 生命周期操作时，应由 kweaver-core 等其他技能处理。 ([tangkelu/kweaver-dip](https://github.com/tangkelu/kweaver-dip/tree/HEAD/skills/bkn-creator) · ★5)

- **blank-sample-contamination-assessment**: Use when a feature-by-sample intensity table includes blank samples (labeled 'blank' in the group-label row) and features must be flagged as blank-contaminated before selecting high-quality features for normalization. - **blank-sample-feature-filtering**: Use when LC–MS/MS feature tables include blank or culture media control samples and the downstream goal is bioactivity correlation or drug-discovery prioritization of true sample-derived ions. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/blas-performance-benchmark) · ★3)
- **blas_lapack**: Complete CBLAS and LAPACKE C API reference (LAPACK v3.12.1) covering 1284 functions for numerical linear algebra: BLAS Level 1/2/3 vector and matrix operations, linear system solvers (LU, Cholesky, LDL), eigenvalue/eigenvector computation, singular value decomposition, least squares, QR/LQ factorizations, and auxiliary routines. Triggers on: BLAS/LAPACK questions, CBLAS/LAPACKE code, linear algebra in C/C++, matrix operations, numerical computing, scientific computing, HPC, linking BLAS/LAPACK. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/blasr-alignment-based-metrics-validation))

- **blast-area**: Map what a set of changes would affect before making it: callers, data contracts, jobs, UI, tests, build toolchains, deploy ordering, and second-order readers — with searched negatives and a list of what the map cannot see. - **blast-based-contig-gene-mapping**: Use when you have a de novo assembled transcriptome (e.g., Trinity contigs from FASTQ reads) and a reference transcript set, and need to identify which contigs are chimeric, correct, or unidentified to benchmark chimera-removal pipelines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/blast-based-contig-gene-mapping))
- **blast-based-reference-database-filtering**: Use when a real metagenomic sample must be reduced to reads mappable to known references before benchmarking classifiers, and when a customized reference database with per-genome taxonomy must be derived from those BLAST hits. - **blast-based-sequence-alignment**: Use when protein-coding transcript translations must be linked to reference protein sequences (e.g., GENCODE Basic translations vs. UniProtKB/Swiss-Prot human canonical or isoform entries) to build genomic-to-protein position mappings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/blast-based-sequence-alignment))
- **blast-based-sequence-similarity-verification**: Use when a kmer-signature classifier (e.g. PlasFlow) has binned assembly contigs as plasmids and you need sequence-level confirmation against known, manually curated plasmid references. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/blast-based-sequence-similarity-verification))
- **blast-based-taxonomic-classification**: Use when OTU representative sequences need taxonomic labels via sequence-similarity search against NCBI RefSeq, or when comparing assignment consistency across 16S reference databases (Greengenes vs SILVA vs NCBI RefSeq). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/blast-based-taxonomic-classification))
- **blast-contig-verification-against-viral-references**: Use when extended or seed contigs from viral metagenomic assembly must be validated — after ContigExtender extension, to confirm non-chimeric, accurate sequence and assign the correct reference virus. - **blast-database-construction**: Use when running SynTracker's first stage: you have per-sample metagenomic assemblies or isolate genomes in a target directory and need them indexed as a BLAST database for homologous-region retrieval against reference genome fragments. - **blast-database-construction**: Use when a pipeline needs a searchable nucleotide reference from a large multi-genome FASTA (e.g. the GeneMark-HM pan-genome) for similarity-based contig-to-pan-genome assignment or gene-to-reference matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/blast-database-construction--eb493a225da1c3e5))
- **blast-estimate**: Partial Skill: invoke by name only — blast-estimate's engine that computes a Mission's blast (low/medium/high) from its touch-set plus the project corpus and lines it up against the hand-typed declared blast — the engine for the blast-estimate node, consumed by the mission-graph's writer. Not triggered by users directly. ([cyberuni/cyber-sdd](https://github.com/cyberuni/cyber-sdd/tree/HEAD/plugins/sdd/skills/blast-estimate) · ★1)
- **blast-evalue-annotation**: Use when a long-read (MinION/PacBio) mapping pipeline needs per-alignment statistical confidence (E-value, MAPQ) written into a SAM file so alignments can be ranked, filtered, or compared against BLAST-like sensitivity benchmarks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/blast-evalue-annotation))
- **blast-evalue-threshold-interpretation**: Use when a set of nucleotide sequences (e.g. a FASTA catalogue of ISs) must be classified against a reference database and the goal is a reproducible strict/loose/novel homology split based on BLAST e-value thresholds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/blast-evalue-threshold-interpretation))
- **blast-forecasting-eval**: Evaluates the zero-shot forecasting capability of universal time series models across multiple domains and prediction horizons. It probes how well pre-trained models generalize to unseen datasets and measures predictive accuracy using standard error metrics. Use when the user wants to benchmark on ETTh1, ETTh2, ETTm1, ETTm2, Weather, GlobalTemp, GIFT-Eval, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports MSE, MAE. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/blast-forecasting-eval) · ★3)
- **blast-gene-id-mapping**: Use when merging or comparing genome-scale metabolic reconstructions generated by different pipelines whose draft models use different gene ID namespaces, and GPR rules must be aligned.
